# Skill Backlog

Use this file as the canonical list of skills to create, improve, or validate in this repository.

## Status Values

- `candidate`: possible skill idea; not yet committed.
- `planned`: accepted skill to create or update.
- `in-progress`: actively being implemented.
- `validating`: implementation exists and needs validation.
- `done`: validated and ready to use.
- `rejected`: intentionally not pursuing.

## Recent Validation Notes

- Colorset1 grayscale publication, 2026-10-05: the source deployment succeeds at the exact release commit; 679 public resources, including 31 exact-commit canonical palette definitions, pass byte/provenance checks with no findings. All 30 committed runtime inventories match their accepted isolated payloads under the recorded newline profile. [Publication evidence and command](evaluations/grayscale-interleave/publication.md).

- Colorset1 grayscale interleave, 2026-10-05: primary red, twelve dark/middle neutral alternations, white, then remaining colors. Thirty isolated runtime owners pass scoped current-source contracts and whole natural cohorts; native allocators preserve both palette boundaries and ordered scalar mappings. Complete attempted evidence, model exception, source binding, readable fixture decisions and publication checks are in [the validation record](evaluations/grayscale-interleave/validation.md).


### 2026-10-05 — Compact connected diagrams with preserved legibility

- Reviewed all 34 canonical bundles; changed fifteen connected-diagram producers, composition flows and the quality auditor. Quantitative charts retain useful data dimensions/scales, source-preserving conversions retain their input geometry, and the nineteen unchanged bundles have explicit scope reasons.
- Default to the smallest passing readable layout: measure full labels, shorten routes and remove excess wrappers/gaps while protecting complete heads, traceable endpoints, independent lanes and meaningful motion. Reject a tighter candidate when it obscures a label, head or route; restore the failed local clearance without reducing fonts or facts.
- Final exact-source registry accepts all fifteen primary contracts and six additional contracts/boundaries, with 40/45 joint strict/native/manual natural passes and at least 2/3 per changed bundle. Retain all five unsuccessful selected naturals and earlier development/infrastructure failures. Ten core bundles use recorded `openai-codex/gpt-5.6-luna`; five consumers use recorded `openai-codex/gpt-5.6-sol`, after Spark was rejected before tools. Strict exact-output, model, read-surface, zero-tool-error and immutable-payload gates are unchanged.
- Measured examples preserve complete information: UsefulCharts area −54.11% and route length −52.55%; composed network node area −14.7% with larger readable text; ECharts concept canvas area −39.6%; video connected-asset footprint −11.97% inside the same delivery frame. These are constrained case measurements, not global-optimum or future-output guarantees.
- Final Slidev ECharts native glyph/backing placement protects labels against every shaft and complete head. Its unchanged 46-file runtime passes contract and 3/3 natural repetitions; actual default click/replay/resize/reduced frames and original-deck regressions preserve fonts, symbols, routes and canvases. Project caption-presence tests reject omission, hiding, undersized text and unrelated legend homonyms.
- Promoted measured-layout recipes, builders, probes and native checks into their owning bundles. The existing [`slidev-animejs-timeline-machine`](https://gvillarroel.github.io/skills/examples/slidev-animejs/#/20) fixture exposes both native click states and retains stationary connections; the rebuilt example passes 129 native animation samples before publication.
- Required pattern-ID, skill, independence and payload checks pass; all 34 source/runtime/full authoring bundles pass (68 copied profiles). Local sync/check matches 10290 canonical files. Pages build and stable catalog checks pass. Source-only staging preserves the pre-existing ECharts gallery and unrelated project changes. Eleven skills return to `done`; the four broader historical `validating` statuses remain open. [Commands, hashes, case registry, evidence and limits](evaluations/diagram-compactness/validation-20261004.md).

- `diagram-type-coverage` and `mermaid-colorset-styler`: On 2026-07-12, Pages runs `29193307188`, `29193946671`, `29194418933`, and `29195096628` exposed unstable long-lived Mermaid CLI 11.16.0 rendering on Ubuntu. Local live validation passed 96/96 with 12/12 first-attempt chunks and zero findings; an independent comparison confirmed eight-item batches are faster than four-item batches. `test_style_mermaid_directory.py`, the chunk-first tests, the combined seven-check gate, `validate-pattern-ids.py`, `validate-skills.py`, `check-repo-payload.py`, the Pages build/format gate, and targeted diff checks pass. - `awsome-videos`: Hardened finished-video visual orchestration on 2026-07-09. The skill now routes frozen URL-backed facts and fact-to-shot mappings, scene composition, semantic transitions, specialist assets, and exactly one renderer through evidence-bound contracts; it scaffolds `source-package.json`, `shot-contract.json`, `asset-manifest.json`, `composition-plan.json`, and `transition-plan.json` while keeping the generic HTML starter final-ineligible. Final package validation now live-reruns the browser renderer, compares fresh renderer frames to the MP4, recomputes readiness, validates current style/quality/motion/capture/audio hashes, and rejects fabricated or stale reports. The 19-check suite passed positive cases plus adversarial path escapes, text/broken binary assets, renamed identical layouts, reversed beats, repeated hashes, mistimed frames/seams, unrelated pixels, inline resource spoofs, broken media, markerless scaffolds, forged renderer/readiness/style reports, and manifest/CLI mismatches. - `awsome-videos`: Added first-class motion-report validation on 2026-07-07. `scripts/render_concept_video.py` now writes `--motion-report` JSON with strong and subtle frame-pair motion metrics, `scripts/check_video_artifact.py` consumes that report, `scripts/score_video_readiness.py` counts it as validation evidence, and `scripts/check_production_package.py` requires motion-report paths/flags in production notes and package validation commands. `assets/templates/production-notes-template.md`, `scripts/scaffold_production_package.py`, `SKILL.md`, and the production references now thread `motion-report.json` through render, video validation, readiness scoring, package validation, and generated manifests. `scripts/test_validators.py` added `motionReportDetection`, where moving synthetic frames pass and static frames fail, plus `scaffoldManifest.motionReportCommandOk`. - `awsome-videos`: Hardened `--require-audio` on 2026-07-07 so silent AAC streams no longer count as finished audio. `scripts/check_video_artifact.py` now runs ffmpeg `volumedetect` by default when `--require-audio` is set, reports `audio_level.meanVolumeDb` and `audio_level.maxVolumeDb`, and fails below permissive mean/max dB thresholds unless `--skip-audio-level-check` is explicitly used for silent previews. `scripts/check_production_package.py` passes the same audio-level controls through complete package validation, and `SKILL.md` plus `references/video-production-playbook.md` document the behavior. Local validation passed `test_validators.py --json` with `audioLevelParser.passed=true` and `silentPassesThreshold=false`; a sine-bed smoke MP4 passed with audio level data while an intentionally silent MP4 failed with mean/max volume at `-91.0 dB`. - `html-d3-anime-video-workflow`: Promoted lessons from `projects/ai-code-assistant-concepts` on 2026-06-27 after a fifteen-video produce/critique/improve loop. Lowered difficulty with exact-output contracts, small bundled smoke templates, and local Three.js vendor files. Passing runtime traces: `mermaid-runtime-flowchart-20260623-spark-6` produced exact `flow.mmd`, `flow.static.svg`, and `flow.animated.svg` with labels and no external network references while reading only `SKILL.md` plus the 165-byte template; `echarts-runtime-bar-20260623-spark-4` produced exact `bar.static.svg` and `bar.animated.svg` with animated marks, labels, and no external network references; `animated-svg-to-gif-runtime-20260623-spark-8` produced exact `pulse.animated.svg` and `pulse.gif`, with `ffprobe` reporting 360x240, 12 fps, 2.000s, and 24 frames; `threejs-runtime-token-orbit-20260623-spark-4` produced exact `scene.html` with only local skill vendor imports, no external network references, and Playwright canvas validation showing a 966x543 WebGL canvas, nonblank colored pixels, 70+ color buckets, changed frame hash, and no failed network responses.
- `d3-animated-svg`: Split all 202 gallery examples into per-pattern references with `references/pattern-index.md`, `references/patterns/<id>.md`, and `scripts/extract_gallery_pattern_references.py`; the original `gallery.js` is 562,936 bytes, while the generated pattern references average about 3.6 KB each, with a 13.7 KB max and a 5.9 KB `force-network.md` after adding a standalone recipe. Added `references/shared-renderer-helpers.md`, a runtime `pi` payload profile that excludes `assets/examples/` for normal skill-use tests, and a reduced-motion visibility check in `scripts/check_self_contained_html.py`. Full-payload Spark trace `d3-trace-force-network-20260623-spark-patterns-3` still read `gallery.js` three times after recursive search found it, confirming that split references alone are insufficient if the large fixture is available. Runtime Spark trace `d3-trace-force-network-20260623-spark-runtime-3` used `openai-codex/gpt-5.3-codex-spark`, had no `assets/examples/` and no `gallery.js`, read only the exact force-network pattern plus focused references, passed the self-contained checker, and rendered a readable Chromium screenshot with 146 SVG elements and 82 animation/style nodes.
- `d3-animated-svg`: Added an isolated `pi` validation path after a skill-only Spark run exposed that self-contained HTML output could incorrectly depend on a CDN and place animated packets over readable text. Moved visual token guidance into the skill as `references/visual-tokens.md`, added `references/self-contained-output.md`, added `assets/templates/self-contained-animated-svg.html`, added `scripts/check_self_contained_html.py`, and added repo helper `scripts/run-pi-skill-eval.py`. Passed isolated `pi` harness with `openai-codex/gpt-5.3-codex-spark` using `uv run --script scripts/run-pi-skill-eval.py d3-animated-svg --prompt-file evaluations/pi-prompts/d3-smoke.md --run-id d3-smoke-20260623-spark-4 --timeout-seconds 900`; passed self-contained HTML checks on the template and generated artifact, Chromium render of the generated SVG at 1000x429 with 63 SVG elements and 14 animation/style nodes, visual screenshot inspection, repo validation with `uv run --script scripts/validate-skills.py`, and payload check with `uv run --script scripts/check-repo-payload.py`.
- Repository-wide: Reorganized all skills under `skills/`, added `evaluations/` for tracked evaluation summaries with ignored local runs under `evaluations/runs/`, moved project-scoped AI concept video production scripts to `projects/ai-concept-videos/scripts/`, and standardized generated project artifacts under `projects/<project-id>/artifacts/` for documents, videos, SVGs, GIFs, screenshots, manifests, reviews, data, and related outputs. Validate with `uv run --script scripts/validate-skills.py`, `node projects/ai-concept-videos/scripts/validate-concepts.mjs`, `uv run --script skills/mermaid-animated-svg/scripts/animate_mermaid_svg.py assets/readme/diagrams/repo-organization.mmd -o assets/readme/diagrams/repo-organization.animated.svg --static-output assets/readme/diagrams/repo-organization.static.svg --duration-ms 900 --stagger-ms 90`, and `uv run --script scripts/check-repo-payload.py`.
- Repository-wide: Moved canonical example and GitHub Pages sources from the old top-level `examples/` tree into each owning skill's `assets/examples/` directory, updated repo instructions and skill references to use those paths, and changed Pages generation to build from skill assets.
- Repository-wide: Added mandatory pattern-promotion guidance to `AGENTS.md` and every existing `SKILL.md`, so reusable examples, fixtures, scene patterns, chart profiles, recording flows, audit rules, and conversion presets must be promoted into the owning skill references before handoff. Passed repo validation with `uv run --script scripts/validate-skills.py`.
- `d3-animated-svg`: Added a self-contained `d3-pattern-parabolic-arcs` recipe for SDLC task dependency arcs so isolated skill-only workspaces can recreate the pattern without reading gallery source. Passed repo validation with `uv run --script scripts/validate-skills.py`.
- `html-d3-anime-video-workflow`: Added `references/scene-pattern-recipes.md` so approved AI concept scene patterns, shared model boxes, beat-module splits, render presets, and validation gates are stored in the skill instead of only in examples. Passed repo validation with `uv run --script scripts/validate-skills.py`.
- `d3-animated-svg`: Added `references/example-pattern-recipes.md` so promoted gallery examples document how to recreate document token quality blocks, extraction buckets, image partial covers, inline bar tables, sketchy overlays, model execution boxes, and token roulette sampling from stable `d3-pattern-*` IDs. Passed repo validation with `uv run --script scripts/validate-skills.py`.
- `d3-animated-svg`: Expanded the examples gallery to 202 cards with `Agent Loop Partial Covers`, an image-backed SVG pattern that keeps a source diagram visible while animated translucent covers and sweep lines partially cover selected regions.
- `d3-animated-svg`: Polished `d3-pattern-document-token-extraction-buckets` by replacing brace-like curved extraction connectors with subtle straight guide lanes from the source page into each bucket.
- `d3-animated-svg`: Expanded the examples gallery to 201 cards with `Document Extraction Buckets`, a single-page document pattern where colored word blocks are scanned in writing order, extracted into filler/correct/wrong buckets, and summarized with calculated unit totals, percentages, and block counts.
- `d3-animated-svg`: Updated the document token quality family so word blocks and row rules reveal in writing order: left to right within each line, then top to bottom through each document, with explicit `data-writing-index` and `data-writing-delay` attributes for browser-level audits.
- `d3-animated-svg`: Expanded the examples gallery to 200 cards with `Document Token Quality Red`, a reusable variant of `d3-pattern-document-token-quality` where wrong spans use the brand red token instead of yellow while preserving exact accumulated word-length ratios, fixed inter-word gaps, and paragraph-like document layout.
- `html-d3-anime-video-workflow`: Added the approved Video 1 final-block lesson for cost handoff scenes: draw model-entering and model-exiting token flows below the enclosing model box so the box occludes tokens until they cross its boundary, and keep precise cost/pricing readouts in fixed secondary panels instead of attaching numbers to moving meter marks.
- `d3-animated-svg`: Refined `d3-pattern-document-token-quality` paragraph composition so word spacing is visually realistic. The layout now packs tokens into paragraph-like lines using a fixed inter-word gap, keeps spaces blank, draws subtle line rules under actual text rows, and avoids leaving tiny single-word residue lines while preserving exact length-weighted ratios.
- `d3-animated-svg`: Expanded the examples gallery to 199 cards with `Document Token Quality`, a reusable document representation where light gray page rectangles contain dark gray rules and variable-length word blocks. The renderer accepts correct/filler/wrong proportions and validates them by accumulated word length, not word count; the included examples cover 20/70/10, 10/85/5, and 70/10/20 using green, gray, and yellow spans.
- `d3-animated-svg`: Cleaned up `d3-pattern-tangled-tree-levels` by removing the redundant low-opacity `bundle-stem` layer that duplicated the vertical portions of active cascade paths. The formerly cyan family route now uses the darker blue hover token, leaving only the real parent-child links visible during the level reveal.
- `d3-animated-svg`: Added approved inline-bar-table guidance for live/current model pricing comparisons: use only current directly comparable rows with verified values, omit no-data rows, avoid stale model families in the visible set, preserve per-column scaling, and use solid bars when comparing magnitude. Repo validation passed after the current video pass.
- `d3-animated-svg`: Simplified `d3-pattern-deep-learning-model-execution` to only render a square model frame with an internal animated MLP. Removed the previous input/output tokens, motion packets, arrows, model text, and border pulse so the pattern focuses on the network activation itself.
- `d3-animated-svg`: Removed translucent informational background fills from `d3-pattern-speculative-decoding-verify`. The verify sweep now uses a solid token color behind the label, token and badge rectangles render with opaque fills, and the reject token uses a darker outline for emphasis without relying on background transparency.
- `html-d3-anime-video-workflow`: Added the approved lesson that recurring visual-language marks must become shared components after approval. The Video 1 LLM model box was extracted into a shared helper and reused by the mechanism segment so adjacent beats preserve geometry, color, internal placement, and activation style instead of drifting through local redraws.
- `d3-animated-svg`: Updated `d3-pattern-inline-bar-table` so embedded bars are scaled against the largest visible value in their own numeric column. The pattern now uses per-column scales and exposes `data-metric-key`, `data-value`, `data-column-max`, and `data-bar-width` on each rendered bar for browser-level ratio audits.
- `d3-animated-svg`: Added stable per-example pattern IDs for every gallery card using `d3-pattern-${id}`. Cards now expose the ID as DOM `id`, `data-pattern-id`, SVG `data-pattern-id`, and a compact visible label; the gallery verifier now fails on missing, invalid, duplicated, or mismatched pattern IDs.
- `html-d3-anime-video-workflow`: Added render-cost tiers for standalone HTML+D3+Anime.js video production: `quick`, `draft`, `motion`, `fast`, and `final`. The `fast` tier keeps 30 fps, CRF 16, and device scale 2 while using cheaper encoding and skipping contact-sheet generation for faster near-final previews; representative 2-second render validation showed `fast` at 9.02s versus `final` at 12.47s, and the `fast` MP4 passed automated review.
- `d3-animated-svg`: Aligned the clean and sketchy Gemma comparison cards to a shared token role map so both use the same repository palette semantics: E4B blue, 31B purple, Q4 neutral gray, VM cost orange, light fills from token highlights, neutral grid/axis/panel colors, and no raw ad hoc colors. Passed targeted browser color audit plus gallery and repo validation.
- `d3-animated-svg`: Expanded the examples gallery to 198 cards with `Gemma Compare`, a non-sketchy version of the Gemma model comparison scorecard using the same sourced benchmark, context, memory, hardware, and estimated GPU VM cost data as the sketchy version. `verify:gallery` now pins `--expected 198`; rerun gallery, served desktop/mobile, targeted replay, and repo validation after this expansion.
- `d3-animated-svg`: Expanded the examples gallery to 197 cards with `Sketchy Gemma Compare`, a model comparison scorecard showing Gemma 4 E4B versus Gemma 4 31B across benchmarks, context, memory, hardware fit, and estimated GPU VM cost. Documented sketchiness as a reusable overlay for any D3 pattern, including scorecards, comparison cards, tables, and explanatory diagrams. `verify:gallery` now pins `--expected 197`; rerun gallery, served desktop/mobile, targeted replay, and repo validation after this expansion.
- `d3-animated-svg`: Expanded the examples gallery to 196 cards with five sketchy variants of existing examples: beeswarm, streamgraph, treemap, connected scatter, and histogram. The variants follow Jo Wood's Plot sketchiness pattern conceptually by using seeded rough marks, double strokes, and hachure texture while preserving the repository palette and standalone SVG animation. `verify:gallery` now pins `--expected 196`; rerun gallery, served desktop/mobile, and repo validation after this expansion.
- `d3-animated-svg`: Expanded the examples gallery to 191 cards with `Pocket Monster Stippling`, a weighted Voronoi stippling example inspired by Mike Bostock's Observable technique and driven by a deterministic analytic creature mask. `verify:gallery` now pins `--expected 191`; rerun gallery, served desktop/mobile, and repo validation after this expansion.
- `html-d3-anime-video-workflow`: Added the approved lesson for active model boxes in narrated diagrams: animate the internal mechanism instead of adding generic glow, adapt existing D3 MLP activation patterns, keep the primary label centered, keep the network secondary, and use soft translucent brand color. Passed repo validation with `uv run --script scripts/validate-skills.py`.
- `html-d3-anime-video-workflow`: Added the approved visual-only production lesson that motion trails should not persist as decorative residue when token/object movement already explains the transformation. Guide lines now must be semantic, synchronized with the moving object, and fade to zero before landing or be removed. Passed repo validation with `uv run --script scripts/validate-skills.py`.
- `html-d3-anime-video-workflow`: Added a visual-metaphor design gate after user feedback that a draft preserved the aesthetic but reused prior diagrams without designing how the new concepts should be explained. The workflow now requires a pre-code concept claim, causal mechanic, candidate metaphors, rejected alternatives, visual vocabulary, and semantic reuse check before selecting D3 examples or reusing previous scene patterns. Added `references/visual-metaphor-design.md`, updated `references/production-loop.md`, and reran repo validation with `uv run --script scripts/validate-skills.py`.
- `html-d3-anime-video-workflow`: Folded in approved AI concept video production lessons for visual-decision questions before each beat, zero-explanatory-text narrated diagrams, explicit layout regions, module-per-beat source organization, user-approval learning capture, and raw-frame continuity checks between adjacent segments. Passed repo validation with `uv run --script scripts/validate-skills.py`.
- `d3-animated-svg`: Added component-reuse guidance for adapting the closest gallery example before hand-rolling concept micro-visualizations, including preserving the Token Roulette Sampler's weighted wheel, fixed pointer, ticks, deterministic spin, and selected-wedge final state when roulette-style token sampling is requested. Passed repo validation with `uv run --script scripts/validate-skills.py`.
- `d3-animated-svg`: Expanded the examples gallery to 190 cards with `Deep Learning Model Execution`, a reusable model-box example where input tokens enter a centered model box while a secondary internal MLP pulses by layer in soft brand red. `verify:gallery` now pins `--expected 190`; rerun gallery, served desktop/mobile, and repo validation after this expansion.
- `threejs-animated-3d`: Expanded the Three.js/WebGL Vite gallery from 6 to 12 scenes with ribbon path, material sampler, network constellation, radial token rings, vector field, and contour layers. Browser validation samples rendered canvas pixels, color diversity, animation movement, replay controls, pointer drag, and desktop/mobile screenshots under `output/threejs-animated-3d/`.
- `threejs-animated-3d`: Expanded the gallery to 24 WebGL cards with D3-informed LLM and matrix-processing scenes for scaled attention, multi-head merge, MoE routing, speculative decoding, RoPE rotation, logit-lens rank paths, SwiGLU gating, and paged KV cache allocation. `verify-gallery.mjs` now expects 24 WebGL cards.
